## assistant

Passage-only implementation complete and ready for browser measurement:

- Reading canvas uses full frame width in passage-only mode.
- Default passage type increased to 18px with 1.6 leading.
- Balanced two columns require at least 64rem of actual pane width.
- Narrow/docked panes use one readable column.
- Smaller paragraph gaps reduce vertical scrolling.
- Saved font scale and accessibility preferences remain respected.
- “Passage only” now indicates current mode through `aria-pressed`.

Player save-safety and study-aid tests: 23/23 passed. Diff check passed. No commit. Desktop fit measurements and responsive screenshots remain with root.

f[key].filter((v) => v !== value) : [...f[key], value],\\r\\n    }));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const toggleDifficulty = (value: number) =>\\r\\n    setFilters((f) => ({\\r\\n      ...f,\\r\\n      difficulties: f.difficulties.includes(value)\\r\\n        ? f.difficulties.filter((v) => v !== value)\\r\\n        : [...f.difficulties, value],\\r\\n    }));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const handPickRows = drillablePassages.filter((p) => {\\r\\n    const searchable = `${p.title ?? \'\'} ${p.firstWords ?? \'\'} PT ${p.preptest} Section ${p.section} Passage ${p.setNumber}`;\\r\\n    if (search.trim() && !searchable.toLowerCase().includes(search.trim().toLowerCase()))\\r\\n      return false;\\r\\n    return matches(p);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  function matches(p: RcIndexPassageWithPt) {\\r\\n    const status = statusOf(p);\\r\\n    if (p.preptest < filters.preptestMin || p.preptest > filters.preptestMax) return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.topics.length && (!p.topic || !filters.topics.includes(p.topic))) return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.formats.length && (!p.format || !filters.formats.includes(p.format))) return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.frameworks.length && !p.frameworks.some((f) => filters.frameworks.includes(f)))\\r\\n      return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.difficulties.length && (p.difficulty == null || !filters.difficulties.includes(p.difficulty)))\\r\\n      return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.status === \'fresh\' && status.attempted) return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.status === \'incorrect_before\' && !status.everIncorrect) return false;\\r\\n    if (filters.status === \'incorrect_last\' && !status.incorrectOnLastTake) return false;\\r\\n    return true;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const pickedQuestionCount = handPicked.reduce(\\r\\n    (n, id) => n + (passages.find((p) => p.passageId === id)?.questionCount ?? ## assistant

No high-priority new correctness bugs found in the builder, launch protections, assignment hiding, footer actions, or shared-frame default.

One passage layout issue: the 64rem two-column threshold is lower than the two 34em columns plus gap require at 18px. Between roughly 1024px and 1269px, the text can become one overly wide column. Raise the threshold to 80rem or reduce column minimum width after browser measurements.

Verification safety correction: `&review=1` on an in-progress session redirects to live drill mode with `review` removed. It is not read-only. Use a completed protected session for strict read-only UI verification. Ordinary completed review also allows second-attempt and flag writes when interacting.

In practice, most keepers were practical tradespeople managing an industrial workplace. A single station required constant fuel measurement, lens polishing, logbook entries, and equipment repair on a strict schedule set by the lighthouse authority. Keepers who missed a lighting time or let a lens cloud over faced fines and dismissal. The job attracted people who wanted steady government wages and housing, not poets seeking solitude. The isolation was a cost of the work, not its attraction.\\", \\"credited_answers\\": [\\"Lighthouse keepers were not romantic loners but practical workers running a demanding industrial job, and the isolation was a downside of the work rather than the reason they chose it.\\"], \\"explanation\\": \\"The big ideas your summary has to carry: (1) popular image: solitary romantics · (2) reality: tradespeople in a regulated industrial job · (3) isolation was a cost, not the appeal. Named traps: Too narrow: \\\\\\"Keepers had to polish lenses and keep logbooks.\\\\\\" Topic-only: \\\\\\"Lighthouse keeping was a hard job.\\\\\\"\\", \\"category\\": \\"Tier 1: One clear contrast, plain vocabulary\\"}, {\\"order\\": 2, \\"prompt\\": \\"Municipal fountains in nineteenth-century cities are commonly described as decorative luxuries commissioned by vain city councils. That description misses their sanitary purpose. Before household plumbing became widespread, public fountains were often the only reliable source of clean drinking water in crowded districts, and their placement closely tracked outbreaks of waterborne disease. Councils built them where wells had been contaminated, and engineers designed the basins to keep animals and washing away from the drinking spouts. The ornament was real, but it decorated a piece of public health infrastructure.\\", \\"credited_answers\\": [\\"Nineteenth-century public fountains were not just decoration; they were public health infrastructure that supplied clean drinking water to districts where wells were contaminated.\\"], \\"explanation\\": \\"The big ideas your summary has to carry: (1) common view: decorative vanity projects · (2) actual function: clean water / disease prevention · (3) design and placement served the sanitary purpose. Named traps: Too narrow: \\\\\\"Fountain basins kept animals away from the spouts.\\\\\\" Topic-only: \\\\\\"Cities built many fountains in the 1800s.\\\\\\"\\", \\"category\\": \\"Tier 1: One clear contrast, plain vocabulary\\"}, {\\"order\\": 3, \\"prompt\\": \\"The switchboard operators who connected early telephone calls are sometimes dismissed as unskilled labor eliminated by obvious automation. The record suggests otherwise. Operators memorized hundreds of line assignments, diagnosed faults by ear, and managed emergency traffic during storms and fires when call volume spiked beyond anything the equipment was designed for. Telephone companies resisted automatic exchanges for decades partly because operators absorbed failures the machinery could not. Automation succeeded only after engineers redesigned the network around the judgment calls operators had quietly been making all along.\\", \\"credited_answers\\": [\\"Early switchboard operators were skilled workers whose judgment held the phone network together, and automation only replaced them once engineers rebuilt the system around the work they had been doing.\\"], \\"explanation\\": \\"The big ideas your summary has to carry: (1) dismissive view: unskilled, obviously automatable · (2) reality: skilled diagnosis and emergency management · (3) automation required redesigning around their judgment. Named traps: Too narrow: \\\\\\"Operators memorized hundreds of line assignments.\\\\\\" Topic-only: \\\\\\"Telephones used to need human operators.\\\\\\"\\", \\"category\\": \\"Tier 1: One clear contrast, plain vocabulary\\"}, {\\"order\\": 4, \\"prompt\\": \\"Hand-copied recipe books passed between households are easy to read as quaint domestic keepsakes. Historians of food increasingly treat them as working technical documents. Copyists corrected quantities that failed, noted substitutions for scarce ingredients, and recorded which preparations preserved food safely through winter. Because the books moved between kitchens with the people who used them, their marginal notes preserve a record of trial, error, and adaptation that printed cookbooks of the period, written for idealized kitchens, deliberately smoothed over.\\", \\"credited_answers\\": [\\"Hand-copied recipe books were not just keepsakes but working technical records whose corrections and notes captured real kitchen experimentation that printed cookbooks left out.\\"], \\"explanation\\": \\"The big ideas your summary has to carry: (1) quaint-keepsake view · (2) reality: corrected, tested technical documents · (3) they preserve trial-and-error evidence printed books smoothed over. Named traps: Too narrow: \\\\\\"People wrote substitutions in the margins.\\\\\\" Topic-only: \\\\\\"Old recipe books are historically interesting.\\\\\\"\\", \\"category\\": \\"Tier 1: One clear contrast, plain vocabulary\\"}, {\\"order\\": 5, \\"prompt\\": \\"The physician credited with early anesthesia records is often celebrated simply for using ether during surgery. Her more lasting contribution was procedural. \'react-router-dom\';\\r\\nimport { deleteSession, getSessionAttempts, listSessions } from \'../api/rcProgress\';\\r\\nimport { REVEAL_LABELS, type RcDrillSession } from \'../lib/rcTypes\';\\r\\nimport { timedSectionResumeHref } from \'@/lib/timedSectionAdoption\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * A student\'s RC drills: resume an unfinished one, open the results of a\\r\\n * finished one, delete either. Self-loading, because it is mounted in two\\r\\n * places (the standalone /rc surface and the drill hub\'s RC practice bay) and\\r\\n * threading sessions through both callers would give the two a way to disagree\\r\\n * about what has been drilled.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Scores count FIRST attempts only, so this row and the results page can never\\r\\n * report different numbers for the same session.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function RcSessionHistory({ tone = \'light\' }: { tone?: \'light\' | \'dark\' }) {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const [sessions, setSessions] = React.useState<RcDrillSession[]>([]);\\r\\n  const [scores, setScores] = React.useState<Map<string, { correct: number; total: number }>>(\\r\\n    new Map(),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const [error, setError] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const load = React.useCallback(async (signal?: { cancelled: boolean }) => {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const list = await listSessions();\\r\\n      if (signal?.cancelled) return;\\r\\n      setSessions(list);\\r\\n      const done = list.filter((x) => x.status === \'completed\');\\r\\n      const pairs = await Promise.all(\\r\\n        done.map(async (x) => {\\r\\n          const rows = (await getSessionAttempts(x.id)).filter((a) => a.isFirstAttempt);\\r\\n          return [\\r\\n            x.id,\\r\\n            { correct: rows.filter((a) => a.isCorrect === true).length, total: rows.length },\\r\\n          ] as const;\\r\\n        }),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      if (!signal?.cancelled) setScores(new Map(pairs));\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      /* Not \\"no drills yet\\". A failed query — outage, RLS, a missing table —\\r\\n         is a different thing from an empty history, and rendering the empty\\r\\n         state for it tells a student their work is gone. */\\r\\n      if (!signal?.cancelled) setError(true);\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      if (!signal?.cancelled) setLoaded(true);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const signal = { cancelled: false };\\r\\n    void load(signal);\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      signal.cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [load]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const remove = async (id: string) => {\\r\\n    const ok = window.confirm(\\r\\n      \'Delete this drill? Its answers and timings are deleted with it, and that cannot be undone.\',\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    if (!ok) return;\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      await deleteSession(id);\\r\\n      setSessions(await listSessions());\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      setError(true);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <p className=\\"rc-empty\\">\\r\\n        Your reading drills could not be loaded. Reload the page to try again.\\r\\n      </p>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (loaded && sessions.length === 0) {\\r\\n    return <p className=\\"rc-empty\\">No reading drills yet.</p>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className={tone === \'dark\' ? \'rc-history rc-embedded\' : \'rc-history\'}>\\r\\n      {sessions.map((s) => (\\r\\n        <div\\r\\n          key={s.id}\\r\\n          className={`rc-session-row${s.excludedFromAnalytics ? \' rc-session-excluded\' : \'\'}`}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <span>\\r\\n            {s.passageIds.length} passage{s.passageIds.length === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} ·{\' \'}\\r\\n            {s.config.timing.mode === \'unlimited\' ? \'untimed\' : \'timed\'} ·{\' \'}\\r\\n            {REVEAL_LABELS[s.config.reveal] ?? \'Feedback timing not recorded\'}\\r\\n          </span>\\r\\n          <span className=\\"rc-status-chip\\">\\r\\n            {s.status === \'in_progress\' ? \'in progress\' : s.status}\\r\\n          </span>\\r\\n          {scores.has(s.id) && (\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Row types for the three RC progress tables.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * These live here, not in src/integrations/supabase/types.ts, because that file\\r\\n * is generated and must not be hand-edited. Until it is regenerated, the RC\\r\\n * tables are unknown to the typed client, so `rcDb` casts it once, in one place,\\r\\n * rather than scattering `as any` through the API layer.\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport type { SupabaseClient } from \'@supabase/supabase-js\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RcDrillSessionRow {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  user_id: string;\\r\\n  config: unknown;\\r\\n  passage_ids: number[];\\r\\n  status: string;\\r\\n  excluded_from_analytics: boolean;\\r\\n  started_at: string;\\r\\n  completed_at: string | null;\\r\\n  practice_test_run_id?: string | null;\\r\\n  practice_test_leg?: number | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RcAttemptRow {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  session_id: string;\\r\\n  user_id: string;\\r\\n  question_id: number;\\r\\n  passage_id: number;\\r\\n  selected: string | null;\\r\\n  is_correct: boolean | null;\\r\\n  time_ms: number;\\r\\n  flagged: boolean;\\r\\n  eliminations: string[];\\r\\n  is_first_attempt: boolean;\\r\\n  created_at: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"15be5b","wall_time_seconds":0.6684928,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1203,"output":"-- Teacher/admin read access to student work.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- Every table below is student-owned and, until now, student-only: the SELECT\\r\\n-- policies all resolve to \\"the row is mine\\". That is correct for students and\\r\\n-- it is why the teacher workspace could not show a student\'s sittings — the\\r\\n-- admin account\'s queries came back empty rather than denied, which reads as\\r\\n-- \\"this student has done nothing\\" and is the worst possible failure mode.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- This adds ONE more way to read each table: being an admin. Nothing else\\r\\n-- changes. In particular:\\r\\n--   * No INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE policy is touched. An admin can read a\\r\\n--     student\'s work and cannot write, edit, or delete it — a teacher\'s view\\r\\n--     of a sitting must not be able to alter the sitting.\\r\\n--   * Admin is `public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\')`, the security-definer\\r\\n--     function already used for role checks, not a profile column a student\\r\\n--     could ever hold.\\r\\n--   * Students gain nothing. Their own policies are untouched and still the\\r\\n--     only way a non-admin reads anything.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- Tables are guarded with to_regclass so this migration is safe against an\\r\\n-- environment that does not have all of them.\\r\\n\\r\\ndo $$\\r\\ndeclare\\r\\n  t text;\\r\\n  tables text[] := array[\\r\\n    \'section_history\',        -- every section sat, with its per-question record\\r\\n    \'attempts\',               -- every question answered, anywhere on the platform\\r\\n    \'wrong_answer_journal\',   -- every miss, with the choice made\\r\\n    \'rc_attempts\',            -- reading comprehension, same shape\\r\\n    \'rc_drill_sessions\',\\r\\n    \'daily_stats\',            -- usage: questions and time spent, per day\\r\\n    \'question_usage\',\\r\\n    \'question_views\',\\r\\n    \'practice_test_runs\',     -- full practice tests and their legs\\r\\n    \'bootcamp_kv\'             -- bootcamp progress, guided drills, resume rows\\r\\n  ];\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  foreach t in array tables loop\\r\\n    if to_regclass(\'public.\' || t) is null then\\r\\n      raise notice \'skipping %, not present in this database\', t;\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    end if;\\r\\n\\r\\n    execute format(\\r\\n      \'drop policy if exists %I on public.%I\',\\r\\n      \'admins_read_\' || t, t\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    execute format(\\r\\n      \'create policy %I on public.%I for select to authenticated using (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'\'admin\'\'))\',\\r\\n      \'admins_read_\' || t, t\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  end loop;\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Verification. Each of these should return the admin policy alongside\\r\\n-- whatever student policy the table already had:\\r\\n--   select polname, polcmd from pg_policy\\r\\n--    where polrelid = \'public.section_history\'::regclass;\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- And this must return nothing — no admin write path was opened:\\r\\n--   select polname from pg_policy\\r\\n--    where polname like \'admins_read_%\' and polcmd <> \'r\';\\r\\ncreate table if not exists rc_drill_sessions (\\r\\n  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\\r\\n  config jsonb not null,\\r\\n  passage_ids int[] not null,\\r\\n  status text not null default \'in_progress\',   -- in_progress | completed | abandoned\\r\\n  excluded_from_analytics boolean not null default false,\\r\\n  started_at timestamptz not null default now(),\\r\\n  completed_at timestamptz\\r\\n);\\r\\ncreate table if not exists rc_attempts (\\r\\n  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  session_id uuid not null references rc_drill_sessions(id) on delete cascade,\\r\\n  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\\r\\n  question_id int not null,\\r\\n  passage_id int not null,\\r\\n  selected text,                                 -- A-E or null (skipped)\\r\\n  is_correct boolean,\\r\\n  time_ms int not null default 0,\\r\\n  flagged boolean not null default false,\\r\\n  eliminations text[] not null default \'{}\',\\r\\n  is_first_attempt boolean not null default true,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz not null default now()\\r\\n);\\r\\ncreate table if not exists rc_annotations (\\r\\n  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\\r\\n  passage_id int not null,\\r\\n  highlights jsonb not null default \'[]\',\\r\\n  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),\\r\\n  primary key (user_id, passage_id)\\r\\n);\\r\\nalter table rc_drill_sessions enable row level security;\\r\\nalter table rc_attempts enable row level security;\\r\\nalter table rc_annotations enable row level security;\\r\\ncreate policy \\"own sessions\\" on rc_drill_sessions for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);\\r\\ncreate policy \\"own attempts\\" on rc_attempts for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);\\r\\ncreate policy \\"own annotations\\" on rc_annotations for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);\\r\\ncreate index if not exists rc_attempts_user_q_idx on rc_attempts (user_id, question_id);\\r\\ncreate index if not exists rc_attempts_session_idx on rc_attempts (session_id);\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

The review failure comes from inconsistent ownership filtering:

- `rcProgress.ts:152` lists every session readable through RLS.
- Admin RLS permits reading all students’ sessions.
- `RcSessionHistory` labels that list “Your drills.”
- `RcResults.tsx:90` displays any readable session.
- `RcPlayer.tsx:217` correctly requires the session owner to match the signed-in user.

Smallest fix: add actor ownership filtering to the personal `listSessions()` query. Keep player ownership protection. Teacher review requires a separate explicitly read-only path.

No edits made for this issue. Root already applied the 80rem column threshold.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"7e4333","wall_time_seconds":1.5600730999999999,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":3744,"output":"import * as React from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { SECTION_DIRECTIONS } from \\"@/lib/sectionDirections\\";\\r\\nimport { FileText } from \\"lucide-react\\";\\r\\nimport type { HighlightColor } from \\"@/lib/highlightUtils\\";\\r\\nimport { cn } from \\"@/lib/utils\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  Dialog,\\r\\n  DialogContent,\\r\\n  DialogHeader,\\r\\n  DialogTitle,\\r\\n  DialogDescription,\\r\\n} from \\"@/components/ui/dialog\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  Popover,\\r\\n  PopoverContent,\\r\\n  PopoverTrigger,\\r\\n} from \\"@/components/ui/popover\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  Tooltip,\\r\\n  TooltipContent,\\r\\n  TooltipProvider,\\r\\n  TooltipTrigger,\\r\\n} from \\"@/components/ui/tooltip\\";\\r\\n\\r\\ntype HighlightMode = \\"none\\" | \\"yellow\\" | \\"pink\\" | \\"orange\\" | \\"underline\\" | \\"erase\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const FONT_SCALES = [\\r\\n  { key: \\"sm\\", label: \\"Small\\", value: 0.9 },\\r\\n  { key: \\"md\\", label: \\"Default\\", value: 1.0 },\\r\\n  { key: \\"lg\\", label: \\"Large\\", value: 1.15 },\\r\\n  { key: \\"xl\\", label: \\"Extra Large\\", value: 1.3 },\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface ToolbarDirections {\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  body: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n  /** Screen-reader description of the dialog. */\\r\\n  srDescription: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The official LR section directions. Kept here as the default so the LR\\r\\n * player\'s call sites stay unchanged; RC passes its own set.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const LR_DIRECTIONS: ToolbarDirections = {\\r\\n  title: \\"Directions\\",\\r\\n  srDescription: \\"Directions for the LSAT Logical Reasoning section.\\",\\r\\n  // LSAC\'s current digital wording, the same text the section\'s directions\\r\\n  // page shows. The old paper-test version told students to blacken a space\\r\\n  // on an answer sheet they do not have.\\r\\n  body: <p>{SECTION_DIRECTIONS.lr}</p>,\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface FontScaleOption {\\r\\n  key: string;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  value: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Props {\\r\\n  highlightMode: HighlightMode;\\r\\n  setHighlightMode: (mode: HighlightMode) => void;\\r\\n  /** Pre-formatted timer label, e.g. \\"20:41\\". Empty string when no timer. */\\r\\n  timerLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** Actual timer mode — drives label prefix exactly. Undefined hides timer box. */\\r\\n  timerMode?: \\"countdown\\" | \\"stopwatch\\";\\r\\n  /** Paint the timer as overrun. A countdown that has passed zero keeps going. */\\r\\n  timerOverBudget?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Fraction of the section\'s time still left, 0..1. Drives the depleting bar\\r\\n   * the official interface paints under the clock. Omit for a stopwatch or an\\r\\n   * untimed section and the bar is not rendered — there is nothing to deplete.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  timerProgress?: number;\\r\\n  /** Controlled Find Text value. */\\r\\n  searchTerm: string;\\r\\n  setSearchTerm: (s: string) => void;\\r\\n  /** Active font scale value (1.0 = default). */\\r\\n  fontScale: number;\\r\\n  setFontScale: (n: number) => void;\\r\\n  /** Show the notes toggle icon (Smart Drill only). */\\r\\n  notesButtonVisible?: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Whether the notes panel is currently open (drives the active state). */\\r\\n  notesOpen?: boolean;\\r\\n  onToggleNotes?: () => void;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Directions dialog contents. Defaults to the LR section directions; pass a\\r\\n   * different set for another section, or `null` to hide the button entirely.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  directions?: ToolbarDirections | null;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * When set, the Directions button calls this instead of opening the dialog.\\r\\n   * A full section uses it to swap the player for LawHub\'s directions screen,\\r\\n   * clock still running, rather than a pop-up over the question.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  onOpenDirections?: () => void;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Rendered in the left group between Directions and Find Text — the slot RC\\r\\n   * uses for \\"Passage Only View\\", which sits there in the official interface.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  leftTools?: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Rendered in the right cluster after the text controls and before the timer.\\r\\n   * RC puts its find-match readout and the next/previous match buttons here.\\r\\n   * (This used to claim response masking, a review grid and full screen, none\\r\\n   * of which RC has ever passed.)\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  rightTools?: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Hide the underline/marker/eraser group. Only pass false for a surface that\\r\\n   * has no text to annotate — the tools are part of the official look.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  highlightToolsVisible?: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Override the font-size options. Defaults to FONT_SCALES. */\\r\\n  fontScaleOptions?: readonly FontScaleOption[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Extra controls appended inside the Aa popover beneath a divider. RC puts\\r\\n   * line height and color scheme here so text settings stay in one control.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  a11yExtraSections?: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * LR ⇄ RC section switch shown at the far left of the toolbar. Lets a student\\r\\n   * jump straight from a Logical Reasoning drill into the RC passages and back.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  sectionToggle?: {\\r\\n    active: \\"lr\\" | \\"rc\\";\\r\\n    onSelect: (section: \\"lr\\" | \\"rc\\") => void;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Bookmark and study-mark controls, rendered at the end of the right\\r\\n   * cluster. Omit on protected timed canvases.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  studyTools?: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function OfficialDrillToolbar({\\r\\n  highlightMode,\\r\\n  setHighlightMode,\\r\\n  timerLabel,\\r\\n  timerMode,\\r\\n  timerOverBudget,\\r\\n  timerProgress,\\r\\n  searchTerm,\\r\\n  setSearchTerm,\\r\\n  fontScale,\\r\\n  setFontScale,\\r\\n  notesButtonVisible,\\r\\n  notesOpen,\\r\\n  onToggleNotes,\\r\\n  directions = LR_DIRECTIONS,\\r\\n  onOpenDirections,\\r\\n  leftTools,\\r\\n  rightTools,\\r\\n  highlightToolsVisible = true,\\r\\n  fontScaleOptions = FONT_SCALES,\\r\\n  a11yExtraSections,\\r\\n  sectionToggle,\\r\\n  studyTools,\\r\\n}: Props) {\\r\\n  const [directionsOpen, setDirectionsOpen] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const [aaOpen, setAaOpen] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const tools: { mode: HighlightMode; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [\\r\\n    { mode: \\"underline\\", label: \\"Underline\\", icon: <UnderlineIcon /> },\\r\\n    { mode: \\"yellow\\", label: \\"Yellow highlighter\\", icon: <MarkerIcon tipColor=\\"#FACC15\\" /> },\\r\\n    { mode: \\"pink\\", label: \\"Pink highlighter\\", icon: <MarkerIcon tipColor=\\"#EC4899\\" /> },\\r\\n    { mode: \\"orange\\", label: \\"Orange highlighter\\", icon: <MarkerIcon tipColor=\\"#F97316\\" /> },\\r\\n    { mode: \\"erase\\", label: \\"Eraser\\", icon: <EraserIcon /> },\\r\\n  ];\\r\\n\\r\\n  const toggle = (mode: HighlightMode) => {\\r\\n    setHighlightMode(highlightMode === mode ? \\"none\\" : mode);\\r\\n            className=\\"ml-1 rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white px-3 h-8 text-[13px] text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 w-[210px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-slate-700\\"\\r\\n          />\\r\\n          {searchTerm && (\\r\\n            <button\\r\\n              type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n              onClick={() => setSearchTerm(\\"\\")}\\r\\n              className=\\"text-[12px] text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 underline\\"\\r\\n              aria-label=\\"Clear search\\"\\r\\n            >\\r\\n              clear\\r\\n            </button>\\r\\n          )}\\r\\n\\r\\n          {/* Right cluster: tools | Aa + line-spacing | timer */}\\r\\n          <div className=\\"ml-0 flex w-full max-w-full items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto sm:ml-auto sm:w-auto [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden\\">\\r\\n            {highlightToolsVisible && (\\r\\n              <>\\r\\n                <div className=\\"flex items-center gap-0.5\\">\\r\\n                  {tools.map((t) => {\\r\\n                    const isActive = highlightMode === t.mode;\\r\\n                    return (\\r\\n                      <button\\r\\n                        key={t.mode}\\r\\n                        type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                        onClick={() => toggle(t.mode)}\\r\\n                        aria-pressed={isActive}\\r\\n                        aria-label={t.label}\\r\\n                        title={t.label}\\r\\n                        className={cn(\\r\\n                          \\"p-1 rounded-sm transition-colors flex items-center justify-center\\",\\r\\n                          isActive\\r\\n                            ? \\"bg-white border border-slate-700 shadow-[inset_0_2px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]\\"\\r\\n                            : \\"border border-transparent hover:bg-slate-200\\",\\r\\n                        )}\\r\\n                      >\\r\\n                        {t.icon}\\r\\n                      </button>\\r\\n                    );\\r\\n                  })}\\r\\n                </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n                <PipeSeparator />\\r\\n              </>\\r\\n            )}\\r\\n\\r\\n            <Popover open={aaOpen} onOpenChange={setAaOpen}>\\r\\n              <PopoverTrigger asChild>\\r\\n                <button\\r\\n                  type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                  aria-label=\\"Text size\\"\\r\\n                  title=\\"Text size\\"\\r\\n                  className={cn(\\r\\n                    \\"px-2 py-1 rounded-sm text-slate-900 flex items-baseline transition-colors\\",\\r\\n                    aaOpen\\r\\n                      ? \\"bg-white border border-slate-700 shadow-[inset_0_2px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]\\"\\r\\n                      : \\"border border-transparent hover:bg-slate-200\\",\\r\\n                  )}\\r\\n                >\\r\\n                  <span aria-hidden className=\\"text-[20px] font-bold leading-none\\">A</span>\\r\\n                  <span aria-hidden className=\\"text-[13px] font-bold leading-none\\">a</span>\\r\\n                </button>\\r\\n              </PopoverTrigger>\\r\\n              <PopoverContent align=\\"start\\" sideOffset={6} className=\\"w-60 p-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-lg rounded-md max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto\\">\\r\\n                {fontScaleOptions.map((opt) => {\\r\\n                  const selected = Math.abs(fontScale - opt.value) < 0.001;\\r\\n                  return (\\r\\n                    <button\\r\\n                      key={opt.key}\\r\\n                      type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                      onClick={() => {\\r\\n                        setFontScale(opt.value);\\r\\n                        setAaOpen(false);\\r\\n                      }}\\r\\n                      className={cn(\\r\\n                        \\"w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-[15px] rounded-sm hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-900\\",\\r\\n                      )}\\r\\n                    >\\r\\n                      <span>{opt.label}</span>\\r\\n                      <span\\r\\n                        aria-hidden\\r\\n                        className={cn(\\r\\n                          \\"w-4 h-4 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center\\",\\r\\n                          selected ? \\"border-blue-600\\" : \\"border-slate-400\\",\\r\\n                        )}\\r\\n                      >\\r\\n                        {selected && <span className=\\"w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-600\\" />}\\r\\n                      </span>\\r\\n                    </button>\\r\\n                  );\\r\\n                })}\\r\\n                {a11yExtraSections}\\r\\n              </PopoverContent>\\r\\n            </Popover>\\r\\n\\r\\n            {rightTools && (\\r\\n              <>\\r\\n                <PipeSeparator />\\r\\n                <div className=\\"flex items-center gap-0.5\\">{rightTools}</div>\\r\\n              </>\\r\\n            )}\\r\\n\\r\\n            {timerLabelPrefix && (\\r\\n              <>\\r\\n                <PipeSeparator />\\r\\n                <div className=\\"pl-1 min-w-[168px]\\">\\r\\n                  <div\\r\\n                    role=\\"timer\\"\\r\\n                    className={cn(\\r\\n                      \\"text-[14px] font-semibold tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap\\",\\r\\n                      timerOverBudget ? \\"text-red-700\\" : \\"text-slate-900\\",\\r\\n                    )}\\r\\n                  >\\r\\n                    {timerLabelPrefix}: {paddedTimer}\\r\\n                  </div>\\r\\n                  {timerFraction !== null && (\\r\\n                    <div\\r\\n                      className=\\"mt-1 h-[5px] w-full rounded-full bg-slate-200 overflow-hidden\\"\\r\\n                      role=\\"progressbar\\"\\r\\n                      aria-label=\\"Section time remaining\\"\\r\\n                      aria-valuemin={0}\\r\\n                      aria-valuemax={100}\\r\\nGet-Content: \\r\\nLine |\\r\\n   2 |  … 6 -First 121; Get-Content src/features/rc/lib/rcDirections.ts | Selec …\\r\\n     |                  ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\\r\\n     | Cannot find path \'C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\dev\\\\ai-os-projects\\\\lsatu\\\\src\\\\features\\\\rc\\\\lib\\\\rcDirections.ts\' because it does not exist.\\r\\nimport * as React from \'react\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Player display settings from the text-size popover. They persist in\\r\\n * localStorage so a student who needs a scheme or a larger size sets it once.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type RcColorScheme = \'default\' | \'grey\' | \'purple\' | \'violet\' | \'navy\' | \'black\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const RC_COLOR_SCHEMES: { id: RcColorScheme; label: string }[] = [\\r\\n  { id: \'default\', label: \'Black on white\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'grey\', label: \'Grey on light grey\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'purple\', label: \'Purple on light green\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'violet\', label: \'Black on violet\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'navy\', label: \'Yellow on navy\' },\\r\\n  { id: \'black\', label: \'White on black\' },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Percentages, matching the official interface\'s own wording. */\\r\\nexport const RC_FONT_SIZES = [75, 100, 125, 150, 175];\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Extra leading added on top of the pane\'s own line height, rather than an\\r\\n * absolute value. The passage and the question column run at different line\\r\\n * heights (1.7 and 1.6, matching the LR player), and one absolute number would\\r\\n * flatten that difference the moment a student touched the control.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const RC_LINE_HEIGHTS = [\\r\\n  { label: \'Default\', value: 0 },\\r\\n  { label: \'Roomy\', value: 0.25 },\\r\\n  { label: \'Roomiest\', value: 0.45 },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface RcA11ySettings {\\r\\n  scheme: RcColorScheme;\\r\\n  fontSize: number;\\r\\n  /** Extra leading, not an absolute line height. */\\r\\n  lineHeight: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst STORAGE_KEY = \'rc-player-a11y\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst DEFAULTS: RcA11ySettings = { scheme: \'default\', fontSize: 100, lineHeight: 0 };\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Line height used to be stored as an absolute 1.35 / 1.65 / 1.85. Map those\\r\\n * forward so a student who already picked one keeps roughly what they chose.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction migrateLineHeight(stored: number | undefined): number {\\r\\n  if (stored == null) return DEFAULTS.lineHeight;\\r\\n  if (stored < 1) return stored;\\r\\n  if (stored >= 1.8) return 0.45;\\r\\n  if (stored >= 1.5) return 0.25;\\r\\n  return 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction read(): RcA11ySettings {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\\r\\n    if (!raw) return DEFAULTS;\\r\\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<RcA11ySettings>;\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      scheme: parsed.scheme ?? DEFAULTS.scheme,\\r\\n      fontSize: parsed.fontSize ?? DEFAULTS.fontSize,\\r\\n      lineHeight: migrateLineHeight(parsed.lineHeight),\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return DEFAULTS;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useRcA11y() {\\r\\n  const [settings, setSettings] = React.useState<RcA11ySettings>(read);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      // A student with storage blocked still gets the setting for this session.\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, [settings]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const update = React.useCallback((patch: Partial<RcA11ySettings>) => {\\r\\n    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch }));\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    settings,\\r\\n    update,\\r\\n    /** Scheme class for the exam pane. Empty for the default black-on-white. */\\r\\n    className: settings.scheme === \'default\' ? \'\' : `rc-scheme-${settings.scheme}`,\\r\\n    /** 1.0 = the LR player\'s base sizes. */\\r\\n    fontScale: settings.fontSize / 100,\\r\\n    extraLeading: settings.lineHeight,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"1103f8","wall_time_seconds":0.8240261,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1163,"output":"import * as React from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport * as PopoverPrimitive from \\"@radix-ui/react-popover\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { cn } from \\"@/lib/utils\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst Popover = PopoverPrimitive.Root;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst PopoverTrigger = PopoverPrimitive.Trigger;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst PopoverContent = React.forwardRef<\\r\\n  React.ElementRef<typeof PopoverPrimitive.Content>,\\r\\n  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof PopoverPrimitive.Content>\\r\\n>(({ className, align = \\"center\\", sideOffset = 4, ...props }, ref) => (\\r\\n  <PopoverPrimitive.Portal>\\r\\n    <PopoverPrimitive.Content\\r\\n      ref={ref}\\r\\n      align={align}\\r\\n      sideOffset={sideOffset}\\r\\n      className={cn(\\r\\n        \\"z-[1000] w-72 rounded-md border bg-popover p-4 text-popover-foreground shadow-md outline-none data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-2 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-2 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-2 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-2\\",\\r\\n        className,\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n      {...props}\\r\\n    />\\r\\n  </PopoverPrimitive.Portal>\\r\\n));\\r\\nPopoverContent.displayName = PopoverPrimitive.Content.displayName;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport { Popover, PopoverTrigger, PopoverContent };\\r\\n        <em>implied</em> in the passage or pair of passages. For some questions, more than one\\r\\n        of the choices could conceivably answer the question. However, you are to choose the{\' \'}\\r\\n        <em>best</em> answer; that is, the response that most accurately and completely answers\\r\\n        the question.\\r\\n      </p>\\r\\n      <p>\\r\\n        After you have chosen the best answer, blacken the corresponding space on your answer\\r\\n        sheet.\\r\\n      </p>\\r\\n    </>\\r\\n  ),\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Text sizes as percentages, the official interface\'s own wording. 100% is the\\r\\n * LR player\'s base size, so the two drills read identically at the default.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport const RC_FONT_SCALE_OPTIONS = RC_FONT_SIZES.map((size) => ({\\r\\n  key: String(size),\\r\\n  label: size === 100 ? \'100% (default)\' : `${size}%`,\\r\\n  value: size / 100,\\r\\n}));\\r\\nconst dash = (value: unknown) =>\\r\\n  value === null || value === undefined || value === \'\' ? \'—\' : String(value);\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function RcQuestionDetails({\\r\\n  question,\\r\\n  passage,\\r\\n  number,\\r\\n  onClose,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  question: RcQuestion;\\r\\n  passage: RcPassage;\\r\\n  number: number;\\r\\n  onClose: () => void;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const rows: [string, string][] = [\\r\\n    [\'PrepTest\', dash(passage.preptest)],\\r\\n    [\'Section\', dash(passage.section)],\\r\\n    [\'Question\', String(number)],\\r\\n    [\'Difficulty\', dash(question.difficulty)],\\r\\n    [\'Question Type\', dash(question.questionType)],\\r\\n    [\\r\\n      \'Question Features\',\\r\\n      question.features.length > 0 ? question.features.map((f) => f.name).join(\', \') : \'—\',\\r\\n    ],\\r\\n    [\'Passage Topic\', dash(passage.topic)],\\r\\n    [\'Passage Format\', dash(passage.format)],\\r\\n    [\'Frameworks\', passage.frameworks.length > 0 ? passage.frameworks.join(\', \') : \'—\'],\\r\\n    [\'Set Difficulty\', dash(passage.difficulty)],\\r\\n    [\'Set Number\', dash(passage.setNumber)],\\r\\n  ];\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <Modal title=\\"Question details\\" onClose={onClose}>\\r\\n      <table className=\\"w-full border-collapse text-[13px]\\">\\r\\n        <tbody>\\r\\n          {rows.map(([label, value]) => (\\r\\n            <tr key={label} className=\\"border-b border-slate-100\\">\\r\\n              <th scope=\\"row\\" className=\\"w-[46%] py-2 pr-3 text-left font-semibold text-slate-500\\">\\r\\n                {label}\\r\\n              </th>\\r\\n              <td className=\\"py-2 align-top text-slate-800\\">{value}</td>\\r\\n            </tr>\\r\\n          ))}\\r\\n        </tbody>\\r\\n      </table>\\r\\n    </Modal>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* --------------------------------------------------------- confirm submit */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function RcSubmitConfirm({\\r\\n  unanswered,\\r\\n  onCancel,\\r\\n  onConfirm,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  unanswered: number;\\r\\n  onCancel: () => void;\\r\\n  onConfirm: () => void;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <Modal title=\\"Submit this drill?\\" onClose={onCancel}>\\r\\n      <p className=\\"mb-5 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-slate-600\\">\\r\\n        {unanswered > 0\\r\\n          ? `You have ${unanswered} question${unanswered === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} without an answer. Submitting now scores ${unanswered === 1 ? \'it\' : \'them\'} as missed.`\\r\\n          : \'Every question has an answer. text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/components/drill/official/OfficialDrillToolbar.test.tsx\n+import * as React from 'react';\n+import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';\n+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n+import OfficialDrillToolbar from './OfficialDrillToolbar';\n+import { RC_FONT_SCALE_OPTIONS } from '@/features/rc/lib/rcDirections';\n+\n+describe('OfficialDrillToolbar reading preferences', () => {\n+  it('selects a text size, closes the dropdown, and exposes the saved current choice on reopening', () => {\n+    const selectedSize = vi.fn();\n+    function Toolbar() {\n+      const [fontScale, setFontScale] = React.useState(1);\n+      return <OfficialDrillToolbar highlightMode=\"none\" setHighlightMode={vi.fn()} timerLabel=\"\"\n+        searchTerm=\"\" setSearchTerm={vi.fn()} fontScale={fontScale}\n+        setFontScale={value => { selectedSize(value); setFontScale(value); }}\n+        fontScaleOptions={RC_FONT_SCALE_OPTIONS} />;\n+    }\n+    render(<Toolbar />);\n+    const trigger = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Text size' });\n+    fireEvent.click(trigger);\n+    const preferences = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Reading preferences' });\n+    expect(within(preferences).getByRole('button', { name: 'Default (100%)' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');\n+    fireEvent.click(within(preferences).getByRole('button', { name: 'Large (125%)' }));\n+    expect(selectedSize).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1.25);\n+    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog', { name: 'Reading preferences' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n+    expect(trigger).toHaveAttribute('title', 'Text size: Large (125%)');\n+    fireEvent.click(trigger);\n+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Large (125%)' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');\n+    expect(screen.getByText('Current size: Large (125%)')).toBeInTheDocument();\n+  });\n+});\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/features/rc/hooks/useRcA11y.test.ts\n+import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';\n+import { beforeEach, expect, it } from 'vitest';\n+import { useRcA11y } from './useRcA11y';\n+\n+beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());\n+\n+it('keeps a chosen reading size after remounting', () => {\n+  const first = renderHook(() => useRcA11y());\n+  act(() => first.result.current.update({ fontSize: 125 }));\n+  first.unmount();\n+  const restored = renderHook(() => useRcA11y());\n+  expect(restored.result.current.fontScale).toBe(1.25);\n+});\n+\n+it.each([0, -100, '125', 9999])('recovers a usable default from an invalid saved text size %s', size => {\n+  localStorage.setItem('rc-player-a11y', JSON.stringify({ fontSize: size }));\n+  const { result } = renderHook(() => useRcA11y());\n+  expect(result.current.fontScale).toBe(1);\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

Keyed by passage so they restore on any\\r\\n * future encounter with that passage, in this session or a later one.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function useRcHighlights(passageIds: number[]) {\\r\\n  const [byPassage, setByPassage] = React.useState<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>>(new Map());\\r\\n  const [color, setColor] = React.useState<RcHighlightColor>(\'yellow\');\\r\\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Mirrors `byPassage`, but updated synchronously on every write. Reading the\\r\\n   * state variable inside the mutators would capture whatever the last render\\r\\n   * closed over, so two marks made between renders — which is simply someone\\r\\n   * highlighting quickly — would compute from the same base and the first\\r\\n   * would be lost.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const latest = React.useRef<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>>(new Map());\\r\\n\\r\\n  const key = passageIds.join(\',\');\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    if (passageIds.length === 0) return;\\r\\n    loadAnnotations(passageIds)\\r\\n      .then((map) => {\\r\\n        if (!cancelled) {\\r\\n          latest.current = map;\\r\\n          setByPassage(map);\\r\\n          setLoaded(true);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      })\\r\\n      .catch(() => {\\r\\n        // Highlights are an aid, not the drill. A load failure must not block it.\\r\\n        if (!cancelled) setLoaded(true);\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\\r\\n  }, [key]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const forPassage = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (passageId: number) => byPassage.get(passageId) ?? [],\\r\\n    [byPassage],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const write = React.useCallback((passageId: number, next: RcHighlight[]) => {\\r\\n    const map = new Map(latest.current);\\r\\n    map.set(passageId, next);\\r\\n    latest.current = map;\\r\\n    setByPassage(map);\\r\\n    saveAnnotations(passageId, next);\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const add = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (\\r\\n      passageId: number,\\r\\n      target: RcHighlightTarget,\\r\\n      start: number,\\r\\n      end: number,\\r\\n      /** Passed in by the popover so picking a swatch applies that color now. */\\r\\n      chosen: RcHighlightColor = color,\\r\\n      kind: RcAnnotationKind = \'highlight\',\\r\\n    ) => {\\r\\n      if (end <= start) return;\\r\\n      const existing = latest.current.get(passageId) ?? [];\\r\\n      // Same target + same kind is one layer. A highlight and an underline can\\r\\n      // sit on the same words, so they never interact.\\r\\n      const sameLayer = (h: RcHighlight) =>\\r\\n        h.target === target && (h.kind ?? \'highlight\') === kind;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Marking over the same color extends one continuous span rather than\\r\\n      // leaving a seam. Repeat until nothing new is absorbed: swallowing one\\r\\n      // neighbour can bring the next into reach.\\r\\n      let lo = start;\\r\\n      let hi = end;\\r\\n      for (let grew = true; grew; ) {\\r\\n        grew = false;\\r\\n        for (const h of existing) {\\r\\n          if (!sameLayer(h) || h.color !== chosen) continue;\\r\\n          if (h.end < lo || h.start > hi) continue;\\r\\n          if (h.start < lo || h.end > hi) {\\r\\n            lo = Math.min(lo, h.start);\\r\\n            hi = Math.max(hi, h.end);\\r\\n            grew = true;\\r\\n          }\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Marking over a different color trims the old span around the new one\\r\\n      // instead of discarding it, so highlighting three words inside a\\r\\n      // sentence keeps the rest of that sentence.\\r\\n      const next: RcHighlight[] = [];\\r\\n      for (const h of existing) {\\r\\n        if (!sameLayer(h)) {\\r\\n          next.push(h);\\r\\n          continue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        if (h.color === chosen && h.end >= lo && h.start <= hi) continue;\\r\\n        if (h.end <= lo || h.start >= hi) {\\r\\n          next.push(h);\\r\\n          continue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        if (h.start < lo) next.push({ ...h, end: lo });\\r\\n        if (h.end > hi) next.push({ ...h, start: hi });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      next.push({ target, start: lo, end: hi, color: chosen, kind });\\r\\n      write(passageId, next);\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [color, write],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const removeAt = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (passageId: number, target: RcHighlightTarget, offset: number) => {\\r\\n      const existing = latest.current.get(passageId) ?? ## assistant

Code audit for the specification:

| Area | Implemented features | Owning files |
|---|---|---|
| Setup | Adaptive/manual passage selection; title/opening/PrepTest search; topic, format, framework, difficulty, status, PrepTest filters; passage count; untimed/standard/custom timing; three answer-reveal modes | `RcHubSurface.tsx`, `rcAdaptive.ts`, `rcBuilderSummary.ts` |
| Preview | Exact loaded passages/questions; collapsible content; answers hidden; changed settings invalidate preview; shortfall acknowledgement | `RcHubSurface.tsx` |
| Reading | Split passage/question view; passage-only mode; responsive columns; font scale, line spacing, six color schemes; passage scroll restoration | `RcPlayer.tsx`, `RcPassagePane.tsx`, `RcExamPane.tsx`, `player.css`, `useRcA11y.ts` |
| Toolbar | Directions; passage-only toggle; Find Text/clear; match count/previous/next; underline; three marker colors; eraser; text preferences; timer; bookmarks and cross-out reset where allowed | `OfficialDrillToolbar.tsx`, `RcChrome.tsx`, `StudyAidsControls.tsx` |
| Questions | Choice selection; cross-outs; flags; keyboard A–E/F/arrows; answer feedback; expandable answer explanations; passage summaries | `RcQuestionPane.tsx`, `RcChoiceRow.tsx`, `RcPlayer.tsx`, `RcPassageSummary.tsx` |
| Footer/header | Save and exit; Question details; numbered question pager; passage separators; previous/next; Confirm/Check passage/Continue/Finish/Submit according to reveal mode; Pause/Complete/help | `OfficialSmartDrillBar.tsx`, `OfficialSessionStrip.tsx`, `RcChrome.tsx`, `RcPlayer.tsx` |
| Results/review | First-attempt scores; type rollups and filters; per-question explanations; review all/question; drill misses; analytics exclusion; protected-record restrictions | `RcResults.tsx`, `RcReviewLayer.tsx`, `rcSessionDestination.ts` |
| Saving | Account attempts, stable retry UUIDs, clock/position acknowledgement, local pending-answer/clock backups, resume-read guards, save-error pause/retry | `RcPlayer.tsx`, `rcProgress.ts`, `rcPendingAnswer.ts` |

Verified gaps to prioritize:

1. **P1: Annotation writes can cross account boundaries.** `rcProgress.ts:399` debounces by passage ID only. `saveAnnotationsNow()` obtains the signed-in user when the timer fires. A mark made under account A can therefore save under B after a switch. Capture actor ownership when scheduling, cancel/reject stale actor writes, and key pending work by actor plus passage.
2. **P1: Annotation loading can overwrite new marks.** `useRcHighlights.ts:31-43` replaces the entire map after an async read. Tools accept edits before loading completes; a late response can discard them. Failed reads become an empty usable state, risking overwrite of previously saved marks.
3. **P2: Annotation save failure has no user recovery.** `rcProgress.ts:409` fires an uncaught asynchronous upsert; optimistic marks remain visible after failure. Add saved/pending/error state and retry without blocking answer work.
4. **P2: Flag saves lack acknowledgement/error reporting.** `RcPlayer.tsx:779-785` updates the UI and fires `setAttemptFlag()` without recovery. The saved flag can disagree with the visible state.
5. **P2: Line-spacing/color selection state is visual only.** `RcModals.tsx:119-147` hides its radio indicator from assistive technology but exposes no pressed/checked state.
6. **P2: Question details uses stale terminology.** `RcModals.tsx` labels passage fields “Set Difficulty” and “Set Number,” conflicting with the RC context’s passage/position vocabulary.
7. **P2: Directions retain paper answer-sheet wording.** `rcDirections.tsx` tells users to blacken a corresponding space on an answer sheet, although the player selects digital choices.

Persistence boundaries must be explicit:

- Setup preview creates no session/attempt and hides answers.
- Starting creates a saved session.
- Live navigation/confirmation/completion saves attempts and position; timer heartbeats save clock.
- `review=1` on an **in-progress** session redirects to live mode.
- Ordinary completed review can save second attempts and flag changes.
- Protected completed review prevents changes to source answers, flags, clock, and completion.
- Highlights still save personal passage annotations during protected review; preferences and bookmarks also persist separately.

No demonstrably unimplemented toolbar/footer controls found. The footer’s unused `onNext` callback is unreachable because RC uses the primary submit handler and its separate pager. No further edits made.

## assistant

Numbering is ambiguous, not inconsistent within the UI. `rcSession.ts:44-49` assigns a drill-wide ordinal. Breadcrumb, question header, details, and pager display that ordinal alongside official PrepTest/section/passage identifiers. They should distinguish “Question 9 in this drill” from official `question.position`.

PT111 French memoirs is official Q1–6; Bearden is Q7–14. Therefore the screenshot’s “PT111 S2 P1 Question 9” does not identify official Q9.

Torchy Brown PT159 S2 official Q2, question ID10733, credits B in both generated payload and recovered source. The UI preserves letters; scoring and choice styling agree. All 2,150 bank questions have consistent credited-letter/choice flags.

Whether B is substantively correct needs comparison with authoritative question data. Majority response D is not sufficient evidence to change it. No data edits made.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"8265f9","wall_time_seconds":0.8909101,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2464,"output":"/**\\r\\n * RC recovery pull — paste this into the browser console on app.lsatlab.com.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Fetches the answer choices and prompt for every RC question the bank is\\r\\n * missing, and POSTs each batch to the local sink so nothing has to be copied\\r\\n * by hand. Start the sink first:\\r\\n *\\r\\n *     node scripts/rc-recovery-sink.mjs\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Then open https://app.lsatlab.com (any page, while signed in), open the\\r\\n * console, paste this whole file, and press Enter. Watch progress with\\r\\n * `__rcPull` — it prints a line every 25 questions. Safe to re-run: the sink\\r\\n * hands out only the ids it has not already stored, so an interrupted run\\r\\n * resumes where it stopped rather than starting over.\\r\\n */\\r\\nwindow.__rcPull = { done: 0, ok: 0, fail: 0, errors: [], running: true };\\r\\n\\r\\n(async () => {\\r\\n  const SINK = \'http://127.0.0.1:8791\';\\r\\n  const LETTER = [\'A\', \'B\', \'C\', \'D\', \'E\'];\\r\\n  const s = window.__rcPull;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { ids } = await (await fetch(`${SINK}/ids`)).json();\\r\\n  s.total = ids.length;\\r\\n  console.log(`[rc-pull] ${ids.length} questions to fetch`);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const queue = [...ids];\\r\\n  let buffer = [];\\r\\n\\r\\n  const flush = async () => {\\r\\n    if (!buffer.length) return;\\r\\n    const batch = buffer;\\r\\n    buffer = [];\\r\\n    await fetch(`${SINK}/chunk`, {\\r\\n      method: \'POST\',\\r\\n      headers: { \'Content-Type\': \'application/json\' },\\r\\n      body: JSON.stringify(batch),\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    console.log(`[rc-pull] ${s.done}/${s.total} (ok ${s.ok}, fail ${s.fail})`);\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const one = async (id) => {\\r\\n    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        const r = await fetch(`/api/questions/${id}`, { credentials: \'include\' });\\r\\n        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);\\r\\n        const q = (await r.json()).question;\\r\\n        if (!q || !Array.isArray(q.answers) || !q.answers.length) throw new Error(\'no answers\');\\r\\n\\r\\n        const counts = [q.a, q.b, q.c, q.d, q.e].map((n) => Number(n) || 0);\\r\\n        const sum = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);\\r\\n        const answers = q.answers\\r\\n          .slice()\\r\\n          .sort((x, y) => (x.position || 0) - (y.position || 0))\\r\\n          .map((a, i) => {\\r\\n            const idx = a.position ? a.position - 1 : i;\\r\\n            return {\\r\\n              letter: LETTER[idx] || LETTER[i],\\r\\n              text: a.text || \'\',\\r\\n              correct: !!a.correct,\\r\\n              response_count: counts[idx] ?? 0,\\r\\n              percent: sum ? Math.round(((counts[idx] ?? 0) / sum) * 100) : null,\\r\\n            };\\r\\n          });\\r\\n        if (answers.length !== 5) throw new Error(`${answers.length} choices`);\\r\\n        if (answers.filter((a) => a.correct).length !== 1) throw new Error(\'no single credited choice\');\\r\\n\\r\\n        return {\\r\\n          question_id: id,\\r\\n          preptest: q.ptnumber != null ? Number(q.ptnumber) : null,\\r\\n          position: q.position ?? null,\\r\\n          passage_id: q.infoquestionid ?? null,\\r\\n          questiontext: q.questiontext || \'\',\\r\\n          correctanswer: q.correctanswer ?? null,\\r\\n          total_responses: sum,\\r\\n          answers,\\r\\n        };\\r\\n      } catch (err) {\\r\\n        if (attempt === 2) {\\r\\n          s.errors.push(`${id}: ${err.message}`);\\r\\n          return null;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 400 * (attempt + 1)));\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Four at a time with a short gap: fast enough to finish in about a minute,\\r\\n  // gentle enough that it never looks like a hammering.\\r\\n  const worker = async () => {\\r\\n    while (queue.length) {\\r\\n      const row = await one(queue.shift());\\r\\n      s.done += 1;\\r\\n      if (row) {\\r\\n        s.ok += 1;\\r\\n        buffer.push(row);\\r\\n      } else {\\r\\n        s.fail += 1;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      .split(\'\\\\n\')\\r\\n      .filter((l) => l.trim())\\r\\n      .map((l) => JSON.parse(l))\\r\\n  : [];\\r\\nconst recoveredById = new Map(recovered.map((r) => [Number(r.question_id), r]));\\r\\n\\r\\n// Recovering a question adds five choices and nothing else: it was always\\r\\n// counted in questions.csv, and it stays unexplained.\\r\\nconst EXPECTED = {\\r\\n  ...BASE_EXPECTED,\\r\\n  choices: BASE_EXPECTED.choices + recovered.length * 5,\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nconst srcCounts = {\\r\\n  \'passages.csv\': passagesCsv.length,\\r\\n  \'questions.csv\': questionsCsv.length,\\r\\n  \'answer_choices.csv\': choicesCsv.length,\\r\\n  \'question_features.csv\': featuresCsv.length,\\r\\n  \'passage_frameworks.csv\': frameworksCsv.length,\\r\\n  \'taxonomy.csv\': taxonomyCsv.length,\\r\\n  \'passage_texts.ndjson\': passageTexts.length,\\r\\n  \'question_explanations.ndjson\': explanations.length,\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n// closed vocabularies, straight from taxonomy.csv\\r\\nconst vocab = {\\r\\n  passage_topic: new Set(),\\r\\n  passage_format: new Set(),\\r\\n  framework: new Set(),\\r\\n  question_type: new Set(),\\r\\n  question_feature: new Set(),\\r\\n};\\r\\nfor (const row of taxonomyCsv) vocab[row.kind]?.add(row.name);\\r\\n\\r\\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ index src\\r\\nconst textById = new Map(passageTexts.map((p) => [p.passage_id, p]));\\r\\nconst explById = new Map(explanations.map((e) => [e.question_id, e]));\\r\\n\\r\\nconst frameworksByPassage = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const r of frameworksCsv) {\\r\\n  const id = Number(r.passage_id);\\r\\n  if (!frameworksByPassage.has(id)) frameworksByPassage.set(id, []);\\r\\n  frameworksByPassage.get(id).push(r.framework);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst featuresByQuestion = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const r of featuresCsv) {\\r\\n  const id = Number(r.question_id);\\r\\n  if (!featuresByQuestion.has(id)) featuresByQuestion.set(id, []);\\r\\n  featuresByQuestion.get(id).push({ name: r.feature, group: nullable(r.feature_group) });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst choicesByQuestion = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const r of choicesCsv) {\\r\\n  const id = Number(r.question_id);\\r\\n  if (!choicesByQuestion.has(id)) Tag and explanation stay null, which\\r\\n// is the truth: LSAT Lab has no authored prose for these.\\r\\nfor (const r of recovered) {\\r\\n  const id = Number(r.question_id);\\r\\n  if (choicesByQuestion.has(id)) continue; // never shadow the original extract\\r\\n  choicesByQuestion.set(\\r\\n    id,\\r\\n    (r.answers ?? []).map((a) => ({\\r\\n      letter: a.letter,\\r\\n      text: a.text,\\r\\n      tag: null,\\r\\n      percent: a.percent ?? null,\\r\\n      responseCount: a.response_count ?? null,\\r\\n      correct: !!a.correct,\\r\\n      explanation: null,\\r\\n    })),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst questionsByPassage = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const r of questionsCsv) {\\r\\n  const pid = Number(r.passage_id);\\r\\n  if (!questionsByPassage.has(pid)) questionsByPassage.set(pid, []);\\r\\n  questionsByPassage.get(pid).push(r);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- build model\\r\\nconst LETTERS = [\'A\', \'B\', \'C\', \'D\', \'E\'];\\r\\nconst excluded = []; // questions dropped for lacking exactly one correct choice\\r\\nlet nullPromptCount = 0;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst byPreptest = new Map();\\r\\n\\r\\nfor (const p of passagesCsv) {\\r\\n  const passageId = Number(p.passage_id);\\r\\n  const preptest = Number(p.preptest);\\r\\n  const txt = textById.get(passageId);\\r\\n  if (!txt || !txt.text) fail(`passage ${passageId} has no text`);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const questions = [];\\r\\n  for (const q of (questionsByPassage.get(passageId) ?? []).sort(\\r\\n    (a, b) => Number(a.position) - Number(b.position),\\r\\n  )) {\\r\\n    const questionId = Number(q.question_id);\\r\\n    const raw = choicesByQuestion.get(questionId) ?? [];\\r\\n    const choices = LETTERS.map((l) => raw.find((c) => c.letter === l)).filter(Boolean);\\r\\n    const correctChoices = choices.filter((c) => c.correct);\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (choices.length !== 5 || correctChoices.length !== 1) {\\r\\n      excluded.push({\\r\\n        questionId,\\r\\n        passageId,\\r\\n        reason: `choices=${choices.length} correct=${correctChoices.length}`,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    const expl = explById.get(questionId);\\r\\n    const hasWritten = bool(q.has_written_explanation);\\r\\n    // correct_answer is authored only for explained questions; for the rest the\\r\\n    // credited letter comes from answer_choices.correct.\\r\\n    const correctAnswer = nullable(expl?.correct_answer) ?? correctChoices[0].letter;\\r\\n    // The prompt normally rides on the explanation record, which a recovered\\r\\n    // question does not have; its questiontext comes from the API instead.\\r\\n    const rec = recoveredById.get(questionId);\\r\\n    const prompt = nullable(expl?.prompt) ?? nullable(rec?.questiontext) ?? null;\\r\\n    if (prompt === null) nullPromptCount += 1;\\r\\n\\r\\n    questions.push({\\r\\n      questionId,\\r\\n      position: Number(q.position),\\r\\n      questionType: q.question_type,\\r\\n      questionTypeFamily: q.question_type_family,\\r\\n      difficulty: num(q.difficulty),\\r\\n      prompt,\\r\\n    const choiceBlocks = styleSections.map((s) => parseChoiceBlock(s.lines));\\r\\n    const choices = choiceBlocks.find((b) => b.length >= 5) ?? choiceBlocks[0] ?? Choice tags are NOT reliable:\\r\\n    // some correct choices carry no tag, others carry a descriptive one\\r\\n    // (\\"Conclusion\\", \\"Weakens\\"). Only when the line is absent (older corpus\\r\\n    // files) do we fall back to a tag reading \\"Correct\\".\\r\\n    let correctChoice = null;\\r\\n    if (stated) {\\r\\n      correctChoice = stated[1];\\r\\n      if (!choices.some((c) => c.letter === correctChoice)) {\\r\\n        skipped.push({ id: qid, reason: `stated correct answer ${correctChoice} is not among the parsed choices` });\\r\\n        continue;\\r\\n"}'}]

It exits\\r\\n * non-zero on any validation failure; it never warns and continues.\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, existsSync } from \'node:fs\';\\r\\nimport { join, dirname } from \'node:path\';\\r\\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \'node:url\';\\r\\nimport { parse } from \'csv-parse/sync\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \'..\');\\r\\nconst SRC = join(ROOT, \'scripts/rc-source/data\');\\r\\nconst OUT = join(ROOT, \'public/data/rc\');\\r\\nconst REPORT = join(ROOT, \'scripts/rc-source/conversion-report.txt\');\\r\\n\\r\\nconst BASE_EXPECTED = { passages: 320, questions: 2150, choices: 8010, explained: 1602 };\\r\\n\\r\\nconst fail = (msg) => {\\r\\n  console.error(`\\\\nFAIL: ${msg}\\\\n`);\\r\\n  process.exit(1);\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nconst readCsv = (name) =>\\r\\n  parse(readFileSync(join(SRC, name)), { columns: true, skip_empty_lines: true });\\r\\n\\r\\nconst readNdjson = (name) =>\\r\\n  readFileSync(join(SRC, name), \'utf8\')\\r\\n    .split(\'\\\\n\')\\r\\n    .filter((l) => l.trim())\\r\\n    .map((l) => JSON.parse(l));\\r\\n\\r\\nconst nullable = (v) => (v === undefined || v === null || v === \'\' ? null : v);\\r\\nconst bool = (v) => v === \'t\';\\r\\nconst num = (v) => (nullable(v) === null ? null : Number(v));\\r\\n\\r\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- load source\\r\\nconst passagesCsv = readCsv(\'passages.csv\');\\r\\nconst questionsCsv = readCsv(\'questions.csv\');\\r\\nconst choicesCsv = readCsv(\'answer_choices.csv\');\\r\\nconst featuresCsv = readCsv(\'question_features.csv\');\\r\\nconst frameworksCsv = readCsv(\'passage_frameworks.csv\');\\r\\nconst taxonomyCsv = readCsv(\'taxonomy.csv\');\\r\\nconst passageTexts = readNdjson(\'passage_texts.ndjson\');\\r\\nconst explanations = readNdjson(\'question_explanations.ndjson\');\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The recovery overlay, when one has been pulled.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The original extract fetched answer choices only for questions carrying a\\r\\n * written explanation, so 548 real questions arrived with no choices at all and\\r\\n * could not be published. scripts/rc-recovery-sink.mjs collects them from the\\r\\n * same authenticated API; this merges them back in.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * They stay honestly unexplained: `hasWrittenExplanation` false, no explanation\\r\\n * blocks, and the review layer\'s tutor fallback is what a student meets. What\\r\\n * they gain is a prompt and five choices, which is all it takes to answer one.\\r\\n * Absent the file this is a no-op and the build is byte-identical to before.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst RECOVERED = join(ROOT, \'scripts/rc-source/recovered/questions.ndjson\');\\r\\nconst recovered = existsSync(RECOVERED)\\r\\n  ? readFileSync(RECOVERED, \'utf8\')\\r\\n"}'}]

The wrong key originates in the recovered source, not player letter mapping.

- `scripts/rc-source/recovered/questions.ndjson`: question10733 already marks B correct.
- All 548 recovered records omit the explicit `correctanswer` field.
